```diff
-64/8 = 8 heads per GPU, but the aiter ASM MLA kernel requires
-heads_per_gpu % 16 == 0. With tp_size=4: 64/4 = 16 heads, which
```

```diff
+"""EAGLE3 draft model with MLA attention for Kimi-K2.5.
+The ``kimi-k2.5-eagle3-mla`` checkpoint pairs an EAGLE3 layout
+(concatenated [embed_norm, hidden_norm] pre-attention input, fc projection
+over the concatenated multi-layer aux hidden states, single decoder layer,
+dense MLP) with DeepSeek-V2 multi-latent attention. Sharing the MLA layout
+with the Kimi-K2.5 target keeps the draft KV cache small.
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,184 @@
+"""MI35x Kimi-K2.7-Code-MXFP4 aiter MLA backend accuracy tests (4-GPU)
+Tests Kimi-K2.7-Code-MXFP4 with the aiter unified attention backend on MI35x.
+This model uses mixed quantization: mxfp4 for MoE layers and fp8 per-channel
+for attention projections (q_a_proj, q_b_proj, kv_a_proj_with_mqa, kv_b_proj,
+o_proj). The per-channel fp8 detection fix ensures the correct kernel path is
+selected for each layer type.
```

- Reviewed files:

- Motivation: Title: "[Kimi-K3] Use explicit SiTU activation for MegaMoE"; model line: Kimi K2/K2.5/Linear/VL; category: docs/tests/CI; main diff: `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`; technical summary: Covers "[Kimi-K3] Use explicit SiTU activation for MegaMoE"; the main implementation surface is `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py`. File-level evidence, code excerpts, and validation risks are preserved below.
- Key implementation: `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` modified +4/-16 (20 lines); hunks: -141,14 +141,6 @@ def _cdiv(a: int, b: int) -> int:; -487,9 +479,8 @@ def __init__(; symbols: _cdiv, _k3_bf16_gemm, __init__, _forward_mega_experts, touching `_cdiv, _k3_bf16_gemm, __init__`.
- Code diff details:
  - `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` modified +4/-16 (20 lines); hunks: -141,14 +141,6 @@ def _cdiv(a: int, b: int) -> int:; -487,9 +479,8 @@ def __init__(; symbols: _cdiv, _k3_bf16_gemm, __init__, _forward_mega_experts
- Key code excerpts:

```diff
diff -- python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py
@@ -141,14 +141,6 @@ def _cdiv(a: int, b: int) -> int:
-# MegaMoE SiTU sentinel: DeepGEMM 0.1.5.post1+ selects the K3 SiTU
-# activation when activation_clamp == 0.03125 (2^-5: exactly representable and
-# unused by any legitimate swiglu clamp; the host asserts clamp >= 0 so a
-# negative sentinel is impossible). beta=4.0 / linear_beta=25.0 are baked into
-# the DeepGEMM kernel.
-_K3_MEGA_SITU_SENTINEL_CLAMP = 0.03125
```

- Reviewed files:
  - runtime: `python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py` modified +4/-16
- Risk and verification: The diff ships test coverage in `test/registered/models_e2e/test_kimi_k3_b300.py`; future changes in this area should rerun those tests plus a minimal launch or accuracy smoke.

```diff
+and the fact that the strided f_a slice is a legal input to the f_b GEMM and
```

```diff
+"""Unit tests for srt/models/kimi_k3_rocm_quant and the K3 weight-merge guard."""
+from sglang.test.ci.ci_register import register_cpu_ci
+register_cpu_ci(est_time=12, suite="base-a-test-cpu")
+import unittest
+from types import SimpleNamespace
+import torch
diff -- python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3.py
@@ -225,6 +225,15 @@ def _k3_bf16_gemm(
+def _is_unquantized_mergeable(weights: list[torch.Tensor]) -> bool:
+    """Return whether these weights may be concatenated into one fused buffer.
+    _merge_weights_as_views cats .weight alone, so anything carrying a separate
+    scale tensor (per-channel FP8, packed MXFP4) must stay unfused."""
+    dtypes = {weight.dtype for weight in weights}
+    return len(dtypes) == 1 and dtypes.pop() in (torch.bfloat16, torch.float16)
diff -- python/sglang/srt/models/kimi_k3_rocm_quant.py
@@ -0,0 +1,45 @@
```
